The inferred mechanism is that a confirmed value stabilizes movement, whereas denial leaves no support against collapse; only the sequence, not this causal arrow, is supplied. `صَدَّقَ` thus acquires the sense of making a course firm enough to bear action.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000852/B006"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_3f70cd895b1176975dda","text":"2. **Charity and relinquished right as the evidence of truth.** Evidence: `ص د ق` B006 (charity/relinquished right), activated by 92:18 giving wealth for purification and 92:19 no recompensed favor. Structural cue: the same giving economy recurs later with money and no return. Mechanism: صدق becomes صدقة-like: truth takes the form of releasing claim. Abductive move: I infer a lexical bridge from believing the good to embodying it as charity. Reading change: “صدق بالحسنى” is not only creed; it is a non-reciprocal gift practice.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000852/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_5a9738ea888046103a5d","text":"92:8–9 made the earlier confirmation visibly behavioral by supplying withholding plus the exact opposite declaration. The sharper surprise came in 92:11: falling activated `ص د ق:B002/B003` as load-bearing firmness, so “truth” acquired structural support without losing its primary fulfillment sense.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000852/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_6a3796d2e9e45417cdfe","text":"1. **Truth as confirmation of the good/beautiful.** Evidence: `ص د ق` B001 (truth in speech), B004 (confirmation/fulfillment), `ح س ن` B001 (goodness/beauty). Structural cue: belief follows giving and guarding, then is answered by facilitation. Mechanism: the act of giving makes the claim about “the best/good” true in practice. Abductive move: I infer that تصديق here is enacted verification, not only verbal assent. Reading change: the ayah reads as lived confirmation of a beautiful moral order.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_9277e6981b056e9dd5ea","text":"92:18-20 explained how the avoidance happens without making it arbitrary: giving wealth, purification, no repayment, and face-seeking form the route by which the “most guarded” is repositioned. The side-shield image therefore became procedural, not only descriptive.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000852/B004","root_000852/B006"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_a8dcc17fd4f5cad781d4","text":"1. **Confirmation enacted through beneficence.** `صَدَّقَ` anchors `ص د ق:B004` (confirmation and fulfillment), and `ٱلْحُسْنَىٰ` anchors `ح س ن:B001` (goodness/beauty) while activating `ح س ن:B002` (beneficent good action). Giving and guarding immediately precede in 92:5; withholding and declaring false follow in 92:8–9. That behavioral frame also activates `ص د ق:B006`, whose charity branch links truth to relinquished wealth. The packet supplies adjacency and lexical overlap; the abductive move is that giving realizes the truth one affirms. The ayah therefore reads as performative verification of the good, not assent detached from conduct.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000323/B001","root_000323/B002","root_000852/B004","root_000852/B006"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_dc956c17ee49adcf4bea","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"92:6:1\",\"root_000852\",[\"B004\",\"B006\"]],[\"92:6:2\",\"root_000323\",[\"B001\",\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"En güzeli doğrulamak, önceki verme sayesinde iyiliği davranışta gerçekleştiren ve karşılık hakkını bırakan fiilî bir doğrulamaya dönüşür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000852/B002","root_000852/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_f346e19e12dfc940d8d8","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"92:6:1\",\"root_000852\",[\"B002\",\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Doğrulamanın sağlamlık boyutu, seçilen değeri sonraki yolculuğu taşıyan bir dayanak hâline getirir; inkâr edilen yol ise düşüşe karşı bu dayanaktan yoksun kalır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000852/B005","root_000852/B007"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_f4186d147f9aa08fa535","text":"3. **Dower/bridal gift as covenantal beauty.** Evidence: `ص د ق` B007 (bridal dower), `ح س ن` B004 (named elevated bodies/places) and B005 (utmost effort), activated by 92:2 bridal display and 92:3 male/female. Structural cue: unveiling/creation/gift cluster around covenantal transfer. Mechanism: “the beautiful” becomes a pledged relation that costs the giver. Abductive move: I infer social covenant from adjacent display/gift branches, not from the surface word alone. Reading change: the ayah can carry a nuptial-covenantal undertone beside the doctrinal one.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000323/B004","root_000323/B005","root_000852/B007"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_fc74125d3c6a3c09c715","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"92:6:1\",\"root_000852\",[\"B007\"]],[\"92:6:2\",\"root_000323\",[\"B004\",\"B005\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Açılma, erkek-dişi ve armağan dizisi, doğrulamaya bedel ödenen bir bağlılık veya evlilik armağanı tonu verebilir; bu okuma oldukça dolaylıdır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
